  "prompt_tokens": 2478,
  "raw_model_response": "To determine how much money I have received from others on Venmo this month, I need to follow these steps:\n\n1. **Authenticate with Venmo**: I need to obtain an access token to interact with the Venmo API.\n2. **Access Venmo Transactions**: Use the access token to retrieve a list of transactions.\n3. **Filter Transactions**: Filter the transactions to include only those that are incoming (i.e., money received from others).\n4. **Sum the Amounts**: Calculate the total amount of money received from others this month.\n\nLet me start by exploring the available Venmo APIs to understand what I can do.\n\nCode:\n```python\nprint(apis.api_docs.show_api_descriptions(app_name='venmo'))\n```",

      "content": "Great! I have the access token. Now I can use it to retrieve the list of transactions and filter for received transactions. Let me get the current date.\n\nCode:\n```python\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\n\n# Get current date\ncurrent_date = datetime.now()\n# Set min_created_at to the start of the current month\nmin_created_at = current_date.replace(day=1).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')\n# Set max_created_at to the end of the current month\nmax_created_at = (current_date.replace(day=1) + timedelta(days=32)).replace(day=1).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')\n\nprint(f\"min_created_at: {min_created_at}\")\nprint(f\"max_created_at: {max_created_at}\")\n```",

transactions.\",\n \"parameters\": [\n  {\n   \"name\": \"access_token\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": true,\n   \"description\": \"Access token obtained from venmo app login.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"query\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Search query string.\",\n   \"default\": \"\",\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"user_email\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"If passed, only transactions between you and user with this email address will be shown.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value is email address\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"min_created_at\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Minimum created_at datetime to filter transactions in YYYY-MM-DD format.\",\n   \"default\": \"1500-01-01\",\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"max_created_at\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Maximum created_at datetime to filter transactions in YYYY-MM-DD format.\",\n   \"default\": \"3000-01-01\",\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"min_like_count\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Minimum like count to filter transactions.\",\n   \"default\": 0,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"max_like_count\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Maximum like count to filter transactions.\",\n   \"default\": 9223372036854775807,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"min_amount\",\n   \"type\": \"number\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Minimum amount to filter transactions.\",\n   \"default\": 0,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value > 0.0\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"max_amount\",\n   \"type\": \"number\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Maximum amount to filter transactions.\",\n   \"default\": 9223372036854775807,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value > 0.0\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"private\",\n   \"type\": \"boolean\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Filter transactions by privacy.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"direction\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Filter transactions by direction of the transaction (sent or received). Will skip filtering if not passed.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value in ['sent', 'received']\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"page_index\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"The index of the page to return.\",\n   \"default\": 0,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value >= 0.0\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"page_limit\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"The maximum number of results to return per page.\",\n   \"default\": 5,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value >= 1.0, <= 20.0\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"sort_by\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"The attribute to sort the transactions by prefixed with +/- to reflect ascending/descending. Valid attributes: created_at, like_count and amount. If both query and sort_by are given and non-empty, results will be first ranked by query relevance, then paginated, and will then be sorted by the given attribute within each page.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  }\n ],\n \"response_schemas\": {\n  \"success\": [\n   {\n    \"transaction_id\": 1,\n    \"amount\": 0.0,\n    \"description\": \"string\",\n    \"created_at\": \"2019-01-01T00:00:00\",\n    \"updated_at\": \"2019-01-01T00:00:00\",\n    \"private\": true,\n    \"like_count\": 1,\n    \"payment_card_digits\": \"string\",\n    \"comment_count\": 1,\n    \"sender\": {\n     \"name\": \"string\",\n     \"email\": \"user@example.com\"\n    },\n    \"receiver\": {\n     \"name\": \"string\",\n     \"email\": \"user@example.com\"\n    }\n   }\n  ],\n  \"failure\": {\n   \"message\": \"string\"\n  }\n }\n}\n```",
      "role": "user"
    }

  "raw_model_response": "I can see that the `show_transactions` API allows filtering by direction (sent or received) and can be used to get all transactions. I need to get the current date to filter transactions received this month. Let me get the current date and then use it to filter transactions.\n\nCode:\n```python\nfrom datetime import datetime\ncurrent_date = datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')\nprint(current_date)\n```",

## Interpretation Boundary

- **VERIFIED:** recorded prompts, outputs, executed code, observations, task outcomes, and first textual divergences.
- **INFERENCE:** a BEST-C/BEST-S difference is consistent with a memory-specific whole-bank effect.
- **UNVERIFIED:** causal mechanisms after the first logged divergence unless directly established by the trace.
